Levels off / soft / standard / strict, switched with "/honest-jev soft|standard|strict|off" (also "honest-jev strict"); the active level is injected every session by the plugin hooks and shown as a statusline badge. Also runs an on-demand audit of a draft reply or recent claims ("be honest with me", "audit your last answer").
